## Data and interpretation limits

- A player's conditional PTS/REB/AST/3PM prediction is not interpreted as predicting those stats in a game the player never played; DNP is a separate playing-time/availability failure.
- Unresolved final-player-box entries are explicitly unverified, not omitted from the list, not graded as misses and not filled using later projection updates.
- Numeric errors measure original projections against independent official finals. They do not certify model probabilities or justify betting promotion.
- Original V2 and V3 are separate forecast families with different timing/role information; no head-to-head claim is made from these separately averaged rows.
- This archive preserves predictions and outcomes, changing no live model thresholds or frontend selection behavior.
